- **11h01** · ▲ A defesa passou de visitas pontuais (quartas e sábados) para solicitação de visitas diárias, exigindo urgência devido ao estado de saúde do ex-presidente internado em hospital. *Fontes: [CNN Brasil](<https://www.cnnbrasil.com.br/politica/defesa-de-bolsonaro-pede-que-moraes-autorize-visita-diaria-dos-filhos/>)*

### Claude Code 401, 403, 404, 429 or 529: what does each error mean?

![Claude Code 401, 403, 404, 429 or 529: what does each error mean?](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fbzxkcuc6gnnus19l0ayt.png>)

Short answer: 401 is a bad key, 403 is a valid key that isn't allowed to do this, 404 is almost always a wrong base URL, 429 means you're sending too much at once, and 529 means the upstream model is overloaded.
